## Summary: /docs/ Route Setup in Safecast New Map Go Application

Based on my analysis of the codebase, here's how the `/docs/` route is configured:

### 1. **Go Route Registration**

In **`safecast-new-map.go` (line 9701)**:
```go
http.HandleFunc("/api/docs", apiDocsHandler)
```

The application registers `/api/docs` (not `/docs/`), which serves the API documentation.

### 2. **apiDocsHandler Implementation**

**Location:** `safecast-new-map.go` (lines 401-449)

The handler:
- Reads an embedded HTML file: `public_html/api-usage.html`
- Dynamically injects configuration variables:
  - `__BASE_URL__` - Constructed from request headers (X-Forwarded-Proto, TLS status, Host header)
  - `__API_ROOT__` - Set to `{BASE_URL}/api`
  - `__DISPLAY_HOST__` - The hostname being accessed
  - `__ARCHIVE_ENABLED__` - Boolean flag for archive availability
  - `__ARCHIVE_ROUTE__` - Path to archive (defaults to `/api/json/weekly.tgz`)
  - `__ARCHIVE_FREQUENCY__` - Archive update frequency

```go
func apiDocsHandler(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    b, err := content.ReadFile("public_html/api-usage.html")
    // ... constructs baseURL from headers and injects variables
    page = strings.ReplaceAll(page, "__BASE_URL__", baseURL)
    // ... more replacements
    w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "text/html; charset=utf-8")
    _, _ = w.Write([]byte(page))
}
```

### 3. **API Documentation HTML**

**File:** `/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/safecast-new-map/public_html/api-usage.html`

- **Not Swagger/OpenAPI**: Uses custom HTML documentation with embedded CSS
- Responsive design with light/dark mode support
- Documents endpoints like:
  - `/api/latest` - Recent measurements
  - `/api/tracks` - Track listing
  - `/api/stats` - Global statistics
  - `/api/countries` - Country statistics
  - `/api/shorten` - URL shortening
  - `/api/json/{frequency}.tgz` - Archive downloads

### 4. **Static File Serving**

**Lines 9479-9492** handle static assets:
```go
staticFS, err := fs.Sub(content, "public_html")
http.Handle("/static/", http.StripPrefix("/static/",
    http.FileServer(http.FS(staticFS))))
http.Handle("/js/", http.StripPrefix("/js/", 
    http.FileServer(http.Dir("public_html/"))))
```

**Note:** The `docs/` directory at the project root contains **markdown documentation files** (architecture, deployment guides, troubleshooting), not web-served documentation.

### 5. **Nginx/Reverse Proxy Configuration**

**File:** `/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/safecast-new-map/docs/cloudfront-mcp-setup.md`

For production (CloudFront + nginx):
- `/docs/` routes through nginx to the **MCP Server** (port 3333)
- `/api/docs` routes to the **Map Server** (port 8765)

```nginx
location /docs/ {
    proxy_pass http://localhost:3333/docs/;
    # ... headers
}

location / {
    proxy_pass http://localhost:8765;  # Map server catches /api/docs
}
```

The **MCP Server** has Swagger documentation at `/docs/` with:
- Host: `simplemap.safecast.org`
- Base Path: `/api/mcp`

### Key Files
- **Main handler:** `/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/safecast-new-map/safecast-new-map.go` (lines 401-449, 9701)
- **API docs HTML:** `/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/safecast-new-map/public_html/api-usage.html`
- **Nginx config reference:** `/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/safecast-new-map/docs/cloudfront-mcp-setup.md`
- **Markdown docs:** `/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/safecast-new-map/docs/` (development reference, not web-served)

    location /docs/ {
        proxy_pass http://localhost:3333/docs/;
        proxy_set_header Host $host;
        proxy_set_header X-Real-IP $remote_addr;

    # MCP REST API endpoints - general /api/ path
    location /api/ {
        proxy_read_timeout 120;
        proxy_pass http://localhost:3333/api/;

        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;

     1→// Package main provides the Safecast MCP server with an optional REST API layer.
     2→//
     3→// @title           Safecast Map API
     4→// @version         1.0
     5→// @description     REST access to the Safecast radiation monitoring dataset via simplemap.safecast.org — 200M+ measurements from citizen scientists worldwide. All data is CC0-licensed and read-only. Powered by PostgreSQL+PostGIS.
     6→// @contact.name    Safecast
     7→// @contact.url     https://safecast.org
     8→// @license.name    CC0 1.0 Universal
     9→// @license.url     https://creativecommons.org/publicdomain/zero/1.0/
    10→// @host            simplemap.safecast.org
    11→// @BasePath        /api
    12→// @schemes         https http
    13→//
    14→// @tag.name        historical
    15→// @tag.description Historical bGeigie mobile radiation measurements
    16→// @tag.name        realtime
    17→// @tag.description Real-time fixed sensor readings (Pointcast, Solarcast, bGeigieZen)
    18→// @tag.name        spectroscopy
    19→// @tag.description Gamma spectroscopy records
    20→// @tag.name        reference
    21→// @tag.description Aggregate statistics and reference information
    22→package main
    23→
    24→import (
    25→	_ "embed"
    26→	"encoding/json"
    27→	"io"
    28→	"net/http"
    29→
    30→	"github.com/mark3labs/mcp-go/mcp"
    31→	httpSwagger "github.com/swaggo/http-swagger"
    32→	_ "github.com/your-org/safecast-mcp-server/cmd/mcp-server/docs"
    33→)
    34→
    35→//go:embed static/favicon.ico
    36→var faviconICO []byte
    37→
    38→//go:embed static/favicon-16x16.png
    39→var favicon16 []byte
    40→
    41→//go:embed static/favicon-32x32.png
    42→var favicon32 []byte
    43→
    44→// RESTHandler wires all REST API routes onto a mux.
    45→type RESTHandler struct{}
    46→
    47→// Register attaches all /api/* routes and the /docs/ Swagger UI to mux.
    48→func (h *RESTHandler) Register(mux *http.ServeMux) {
    49→	// Historical data
    50→	mux.HandleFunc("/api/radiation", h.handleRadiation)
    51→	mux.HandleFunc("/api/area", h.handleArea)
    52→	mux.HandleFunc("/api/tracks", h.handleTracks)
    53→	mux.HandleFunc("/api/track/", h.handleTrack)   // /api/track/{id}
    54→	mux.HandleFunc("/api/device/", h.handleDevice) // /api/device/{id}/history
    55→
    56→	// Real-time sensors
    57→	mux.HandleFunc("/api/sensors", h.handleSensors)
    58→	mux.HandleFunc("/api/sensor/", h.handleSensor) // /api/sensor/{id}/current or /history
    59→
    60→	// Spectroscopy
    61→	mux.HandleFunc("/api/spectra", h.handleSpectra)
    62→	mux.HandleFunc("/api/spectrum/", h.handleSpectrum) // /api/spectrum/{marker_id}
    63→
    64→	// Reference / stats
    65→	mux.HandleFunc("/api/stats", h.handleStats)
    66→	mux.HandleFunc("/api/extreme", handleRESTExtremeReadings)
    67→	mux.HandleFunc("/api/info/", h.handleInfo) // /api/info/{topic}
    68→
    69→	// GPT-optimised compact endpoints (for Custom GPT Actions)
    70→	h.RegisterGPT(mux)
    71→
    72→	// Favicon endpoints
    73→	mux.HandleFunc("/docs/favicon.ico", serveFavicon)
    74→	mux.HandleFunc("/docs/favicon-16x16.png", serveFavicon16)
    75→	mux.HandleFunc("/docs/favicon-32x32.png", serveFavicon32)
    76→
    77→	// Swagger UI — themed to match simplemap admin pages
    78→	mux.HandleFunc("/docs/swagger-theme.css", serveSwaggerTheme)
    79→	mux.Handle("/docs/", httpSwagger.Handler(
    80→		httpSwagger.URL("/docs/doc.json"),
    81→		httpSwagger.UIConfig(map[string]string{
    82→			"onComplete": `function() {
    83→				// Change page title
    84→				document.title = 'Safecast MCP Docs';
    85→
    86→				// Remove Swagger logo completely from DOM
    87→				const swaggerLogo = document.querySelector('.topbar-wrapper .link');
    88→				if (swaggerLogo) {
    89→					swaggerLogo.remove();
    90→				}
    91→				// Also remove any img tags in topbar as backup
    92→				const logoImgs = document.querySelectorAll('.topbar-wrapper img');
    93→				logoImgs.forEach(img => img.remove());
    94→
    95→				// Inject Safecast favicon
    96→				const link16 = document.createElement('link');
    97→				link16.rel = 'icon';
    98→				link16.type = 'image/png';
    99→				link16.sizes = '16x16';
   100→				link16.href = '/docs/favicon-16x16.png';

     1→package main
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"context"
     5→	"log"
     6→	"net/http"
     7→	"os"
     8→	"time"
     9→
    10→	"github.com/mark3labs/mcp-go/mcp"
    11→	"github.com/mark3labs/mcp-go/server"
    12→)
    13→
    14→func main() {
    15→
    16→	log.Println("DEBUG: safecast MCP server binary version 2026-02-18-1")
    17→	// Create MCP server
    18→	mcpServer := server.NewMCPServer(
    19→		"safecast-mcp",
    20→		"1.0.0",
    21→	)
    22→
    23→	// Initialize database connection
    24→	if os.Getenv("DATABASE_URL") != "" {
    25→		if err := initDB(); err != nil {
    26→			log.Printf("Warning: database connection failed: %v (using REST API fallback)", err)
    27→		} else {
    28→			log.Println("Connected to PostgreSQL database")
    29→		}
    30→	} else {
    31→		log.Println("No DATABASE_URL set, using REST API only")
    32→	}
    33→
    34→	// Initialize DuckDB Analytics
    35→	if err := initDuckDB(); err != nil {
    36→		log.Printf("Warning: failed to initialize DuckDB: %v (analytics features disabled)", err)
    37→	} else {
    38→		log.Println("Initialized DuckDB analytics engine")
    39→	}
    40→
    41→	// Register tools
    42→	mcpServer.AddTool(
    43→		mcp.NewTool("ping",
    44→			mcp.WithDescription("Health check tool"),
    45→		),
    46→		instrument("ping", pingHandler),
    47→	)
    48→
    49→	mcpServer.AddTool(queryRadiationToolDef, instrument("query_radiation", handleQueryRadiation))
    50→	mcpServer.AddTool(searchAreaToolDef, instrument("search_area", handleSearchArea))
    51→	mcpServer.AddTool(listTracksToolDef, instrument("list_tracks", handleListTracks))
    52→	mcpServer.AddTool(getTrackToolDef, instrument("get_track", handleGetTrack))
    53→	mcpServer.AddTool(deviceHistoryToolDef, instrument("device_history", handleDeviceHistory))
    54→	mcpServer.AddTool(getSpectrumToolDef, instrument("get_spectrum", handleGetSpectrum))
    55→	mcpServer.AddTool(listSpectraToolDef, instrument("list_spectra", handleListSpectra))
    56→	mcpServer.AddTool(radiationInfoToolDef, instrument("radiation_info", handleRadiationInfo))
    57→	mcpServer.AddTool(dbInfoToolDef, instrument("db_info", handleDBInfo))
    58→	mcpServer.AddTool(listSensorsToolDef, instrument("list_sensors", handleListSensors))
    59→	mcpServer.AddTool(sensorCurrentToolDef, instrument("sensor_current", handleSensorCurrent))
    60→	mcpServer.AddTool(sensorHistoryToolDef, instrument("sensor_history", handleSensorHistory))
    61→	mcpServer.AddTool(queryAnalyticsToolDef, instrument("query_analytics", handleQueryAnalytics))
    62→	mcpServer.AddTool(radiationStatsToolDef, instrument("radiation_stats", handleRadiationStats))
    63→	mcpServer.AddTool(queryDuckDBLogsToolDef, instrument("query_duckdb_logs", handleQueryDuckDBLogs))
    64→	mcpServer.AddTool(queryExtremeReadingsToolDef, instrument("query_extreme_readings", handleQueryExtremeReadings))
    65→	mcpServer.AddTool(topUploadersToolDef, instrument("top_uploaders", handleTopUploaders))
    66→	mcpServer.AddTool(searchTracksLocationToolDef, instrument("search_tracks_by_location", handleSearchTracksByLocation))
    67→
    68→	// 🚨 TRANSPORT SWITCH
    69→	if os.Getenv("MCP_TRANSPORT") == "stdio" {
    70→
    71→		log.Println("Starting MCP server in stdio mode (Claude Desktop)")
    72→
    73→		stdioServer := server.NewStdioServer(mcpServer)
    74→
    75→		err := stdioServer.Listen(
    76→			context.Background(),
    77→			os.Stdin,
    78→			os.Stdout,
    79→		)
    80→
    81→		if err != nil {
    82→			log.Fatal(err)
    83→		}
    84→
    85→		return
    86→	}
    87→
    88→	// Default: HTTP mode (production)
    89→
    90→	baseURL := os.Getenv("MCP_BASE_URL")
    91→	if baseURL == "" {
    92→		baseURL = "http://localhost:3333"
    93→	}
    94→
    95→	sseServer := server.NewSSEServer(mcpServer,
    96→		server.WithBaseURL(baseURL),
    97→		server.WithStaticBasePath("/mcp"),
    98→	)
    99→
   100→	httpServer := server.NewStreamableHTTPServer(mcpServer,
   101→		server.WithEndpointPath("/mcp-http"),
   102→	)
   103→
   104→	mux := http.NewServeMux()
   105→	mux.Handle("/mcp-http", httpServer)
   106→	mux.Handle("/mcp/", sseServer) // SSE server handles /mcp/sse and /mcp/message
   107→
   108→	port := os.Getenv("MCP_PORT")
   109→	if port == "" {
   110→		port = "3333"
   111→	}
   112→
   113→	listenAddr := ":" + port

> AGENT

Found the bug. In `main.go`, the `mux` is created but the `RESTHandler` is **never registered on it**. The REST API routes (including `/docs/`) are defined in `rest.go`'s `Register()` method, but `main.go` only adds `/mcp-http` and `/mcp/` to the mux, then passes the mux directly to `ListenAndServe` — never calling `RESTHandler.Register(mux)`.

The log lines at 119-120 are misleading — they print "REST API: /api/..." and "Swagger UI: /docs/" but those routes were never actually wired up.

200
```

> AGENT

`/docs/index.html` returns 200. The fix was simple: `main.go` was logging "Swagger UI: /docs/" but never actually calling `rest.Register(mux)`, so the routes were never wired up.

The two-line fix adds:
```go
rest := &RESTHandler{}
rest.Register(mux)
```

https://simplemap.safecast.org/docs/ should now work. Want me to also commit this fix in the MCP repo?

> AGENT

No, CloudFront doesn't need changes. The request path is:

**CloudFront → nginx → port 3333 (MCP server)**

Nginx already has the `/docs/` proxy rule pointing to `http://localhost:3333/docs/`, and that was working correctly. The problem was purely that the MCP binary wasn't registering those routes. Now that it does, the existing nginx config handles it fine.

Ubuntu 24.04.4 LTS
ssl_protocols TLSv1.2 TLSv1.3;
ssl_prefer_server_ciphers off;

ssl_ciphers "ECDHE-ECDSA-AES128-GCM-SHA256:ECDHE-RSA-AES128-GCM-SHA256:ECDHE-ECDSA-AES256-GCM-SHA384:ECDHE-RSA-AES256-GCM-SHA384:ECDHE-ECDSA-CHACHA20-POLY1305:ECDHE-RSA-CHACHA20-POLY1305:DHE-RSA-AES128-GCM-SHA256:DHE-RSA-AES256-GCM-SHA384";

# configuration file /etc/nginx/sites-enabled/simplemap.safecast.org:
server {
    server_name simplemap.safecast.org;
    
    # For Let's Encrypt verification
    location /.well-known/acme-challenge/ {
        root /var/www/html;
    }
    
    # MCP Server endpoints
    location /mcp {
        proxy_pass http://localhost:3333;
        proxy_http_version 1.1;
        proxy_set_header Upgrade $http_upgrade;
        proxy_set_header Connection "upgrade";
        proxy_set_header Host $host;
        proxy_set_header X-Real-IP $remote_addr;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto $scheme;
        proxy_read_timeout 86400;
    }
    
    location /mcp-http {
        proxy_pass http://localhost:3333;
        proxy_http_version 1.1;
        proxy_set_header Host $host;
        proxy_set_header X-Real-IP $remote_addr;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto $scheme;
    }
    
    # MCP REST API documentation
    location /docs/ {
        proxy_pass http://localhost:3333/docs/;
        proxy_set_header Host $host;
        proxy_set_header X-Real-IP $remote_addr;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto $scheme;
    }
    
    # MCP REST API endpoints - general /api/ path
    location /api/ {
        proxy_read_timeout 120;
        proxy_pass http://localhost:3333/api/;
        proxy_set_header Host $host;
        proxy_set_header X-Real-IP $remote_addr;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto $scheme;
    }
    

    # Safecast Web Chat (MCP + Claude streaming)
    location /assistant/ {
        proxy_pass http://localhost:3334/;
        proxy_http_version 1.1;
        proxy_set_header Host $host;
        proxy_set_header X-Real-IP $remote_addr;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto $scheme;
        proxy_buffering off;
        proxy_read_timeout 120s;
        proxy_cache off;
    }
    # Map server (including /api/auth/* and everything else)
    location / {
        proxy_pass http://localhost:8765;
        proxy_http_version 1.1;
        proxy_set_header Upgrade $http_upgrade;
        proxy_set_header Connection "upgrade";
        proxy_set_header Host $host;
        proxy_set_header X-Real-IP $remote_addr;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto $scheme;
        client_max_body_size 100M;
    }

    listen [::]:443 ssl ipv6only=on; # managed by Certbot
    listen 443 ssl; # managed by Certbot
    ssl_certificate /etc/letsencrypt/live/simplemap.safecast.org/fullchain.pem; # managed by Certbot
    ssl_certificate_key /etc/letsencrypt/live/simplemap.safecast.org/privkey.pem; # managed by Certbot
    include /etc/letsencrypt/options-ssl-nginx.conf; # managed by Certbot
    ssl_dhparam /etc/letsencrypt/ssl-dhparams.pem; # managed by Certbot

}
server {
    if ($host = simplemap.safecast.org) {
        return 301 https://$host$request_uri;
    } # managed by Certbot


    listen 80;
    listen [::]:80;
    server_name simplemap.safecast.org;
    return 404; # managed by Certbot


}
```

> AGENT

Found it. CloudFront connects to **`origin-simplemap.safecast.org`** (the origin server block), but `/docs/` was only added to the `simplemap.safecast.org` block. The origin block is missing it, so `/docs/` falls through to `location /` → port 8765 → the map.

Let me check and fix the origin server block:

  "description": "Show origin-simplemap server block"
}
```

> AGENT

Fixed. The origin nginx block was missing the `/docs/` and `/api/` locations — CloudFront always routes through `origin-simplemap.safecast.org`, so only that block matters. https://simplemap.safecast.org/docs/ now returns the Swagger UI correctly.

> AGENT

It's **not adding new features** — it's a pure refactoring. All these functions already exist in `safecast-new-map.go`; the branch just moves them into structured packages. Looking at the diff stat: `safecast-new-map.go` loses 748 lines and `pkg/web/` gains the same code split across handler files.

The functions moved are existing ones:

| File | What it contains |
|---|---|
| `handlers_bounds.go` | `apiTracksBounds` — bounding box query |
| `handlers_docs.go` | `apiDocs`, `license` — API docs & license page |
| `handlers_geo.go` | `geoIP`, `geoIPLookup`, `requestClientIP` — geo IP lookup |
| `handlers_markers.go` | `markersWithSpectra`, `updateCoordinates` — marker data |
| `handlers_qr.go` | `qrPng` — QR code generation |
| `handlers_short.go` | `shortRedirect` — URL shortener |
| `handlers_spectrum.go` | `spectrum`, `spectrumDownload` — spectrum data |
| `handlers_trackinfo.go` | `trackInfo` — track info |
| `server.go` | `NewServer`, `Register`, `gzipWrap` — server wiring |
| `context.go` | `WithMinimumDeadline` — context utility |
| `handlers_test.go` | 761 lines of new tests (this IS new) |

The only actual addition is the **test suite** — previously these handlers had no tests.

func trackInfoHandler(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	if db == nil || db.DB == nil {
		http.Error(w, "Database not available", http.StatusServiceUnavailable)
		return
	}
	trackID := strings.TrimPrefix(r.URL.Path, "/api/track-info/")
	if trackID == "" {
		http.Error(w, "Missing track ID", http.StatusBadRequest)
		return
	}

	ctx := r.Context()
	var username, detector string
	var recordingDate int64

	var query string
	switch *dbType {
	case "pgx", "duckdb":
		query = `SELECT COALESCE(username, ''), COALESCE(detector, ''),
		         COALESCE(EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM recording_date)::BIGINT, 0)
		         FROM uploads WHERE track_id = $1 LIMIT 1`
	default:
		query = `SELECT COALESCE(username, ''), COALESCE(detector, ''),
		         COALESCE(recording_date, 0)
		         FROM uploads WHERE track_id = ? LIMIT 1`
	}

	err := db.DB.QueryRowContext(ctx, query, trackID).Scan(&username, &detector, &recordingDate)
	if err != nil {
		// No upload record found — return empty info rather than error
		w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
		w.Header().Set("Cache-Control", "public, max-age=3600")
		w.Write([]byte(`{"trackID":"` + trackID + `"}`))
		return
	}

	w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
	w.Header().Set("Cache-Control", "public, max-age=3600")
	json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(map[string]interface{}{
		"trackID":       trackID,
		"username":      username,
		"detector":      detector,
		"recordingDate": recordingDate,
	})
}

// markersWithSpectraHandler returns markers that have associated spectral data.
// GET /api/markers/spectra?minLat=...&maxLat=...&minLon=...&maxLon=...
func markersWithSpectraHandler(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	if db == nil || db.DB == nil {
		http.Error(w, "Database not available", http.StatusServiceUnavailable)
		return
	}

	ctx, cancel := withMinimumDeadline(r.Context(), 30*time.Second)
	defer cancel()

	q := r.URL.Query()

name: Deploy to Production

on:
  push:
    branches: [ main ]
  workflow_dispatch:

jobs:
  deploy:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest

    steps:
      - name: Checkout code
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Set up Go
        uses: actions/setup-go@v5
        with:
          go-version: "1.23"
          cache: true
          cache-dependency-path: go.sum

      - name: Build binary
        run: |
          go build -o safecast-new-map .

      - name: Setup SSH
        run: |
          mkdir -p ~/.ssh
          echo "${{ secrets.DEPLOY_SSH_KEY }}" > ~/.ssh/deploy_key
          chmod 600 ~/.ssh/deploy_key
          ssh-keyscan -H 65.108.24.131 >> ~/.ssh/known_hosts

      - name: Stop service
        run: |
          ssh -i ~/.ssh/deploy_key root@65.108.24.131 "systemctl stop safecast-new-map"

      - name: Deploy binary
        run: |
          rsync -avP -e "ssh -i ~/.ssh/deploy_key" ./safecast-new-map root@65.108.24.131:/usr/local/bin/

      - name: Start service
        run: |
          ssh -i ~/.ssh/deploy_key root@65.108.24.131 "systemctl start safecast-new-map"

      - name: Verify deployment
        run: |
          ssh -i ~/.ssh/deploy_key root@65.108.24.131 "systemctl status safecast-new-map"

      - name: Invalidate CloudFront cache
        run: |
          aws cloudfront create-invalidation --distribution-id E12FYIQ8RRXOJ1 --paths "/*"
        env:
          AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID: ${{ secrets.AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID }}
          AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY: ${{ secrets.AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY }}
          AWS_DEFAULT_REGION: us-east-1

      - name: Cleanup
        if: always()
        run: |
          rm -f ~/.ssh/deploy_key
name: stable release

on:
  push:
    branches: [ main ]
  workflow_dispatch:

permissions:
  contents: write

# ---------- PORTABLE: всё без CGO и без DuckDB ----------
jobs:
  build_portable:
    if: contains(toJson(github.event.head_commit.message), 'stable release') || github.event_name == 'workflow_dispatch'
    runs-on: ${{ matrix.runner }}
    strategy:
      fail-fast: false
      matrix:
        include:
          # Linux
          - { goos: linux,  goarch: amd64, goversion: "1.23", runner: "ubuntu-24.04" }
          - { goos: linux,  goarch: arm64, goversion: "1.23", runner: "ubuntu-24.04" }
          - { goos: linux,  goarch: 386,   goversion: "1.23", runner: "ubuntu-24.04" }
          # macOS
          - { goos: darwin, goarch: amd64, goversion: "1.23", runner: "macos-13" }
          - { goos: darwin, goarch: arm64, goversion: "1.23", runner: "macos-14" }
          # *BSD
          - { goos: freebsd, goarch: amd64, goversion: "1.23", runner: "ubuntu-24.04" }
          - { goos: freebsd, goarch: arm64, goversion: "1.23", runner: "ubuntu-24.04" }
          - { goos: openbsd, goarch: amd64, goversion: "1.23", runner: "ubuntu-24.04" }
          - { goos: openbsd, goarch: arm64, goversion: "1.23", runner: "ubuntu-24.04" }
          - { goos: netbsd,  goarch: amd64, goversion: "1.23", runner: "ubuntu-24.04" }
          # Windows
          - { goos: windows, goarch: amd64, goversion: "1.23", runner: "windows-latest" }
          - { goos: windows, goarch: arm64, goversion: "1.23", runner: "windows-latest" }

    steps:
      - name: Checkout
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Set up Go
        uses: actions/setup-go@v5
        with:
          go-version: "${{ matrix.goversion }}"
          cache: true
          cache-dependency-path: go.sum

      - name: go mod tidy (POSIX)
        if: runner.os != 'Windows'
        shell: bash
        run: go mod tidy

      - name: go mod tidy (Windows)
        if: runner.os == 'Windows'
        shell: pwsh
        run: go mod tidy

      - name: Build (POSIX)
        if: runner.os != 'Windows'
        shell: bash
        env:
          GOOS: ${{ matrix.goos }}
          GOARCH: ${{ matrix.goarch }}
        run: |
          set -euo pipefail
          export CGO_ENABLED=0
          VERSION=${{ github.run_number }}
          mkdir -p dist
          BIN="chicha-isotope-map_${GOOS}_${GOARCH}"
          GOFLAGS="-trimpath -buildvcs=false" \
          go build -tags netgo,osusergo \
                   -ldflags "-s -w -X main.CompileVersion=$VERSION" \
                   -o "dist/${BIN}"
      - name: Build (Windows)
        if: runner.os == 'Windows'
        shell: pwsh
        env:
          GOOS: ${{ matrix.goos }}
          GOARCH: ${{ matrix.goarch }}
        run: |
          $ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
          $env:CGO_ENABLED = "0"
          $version = "${{ github.run_number }}"
          New-Item -Force -ItemType Directory dist | Out-Null
          $bin = "chicha-isotope-map_${{ matrix.goos }}_${{ matrix.goarch }}.exe"
          go build -tags "netgo,osusergo" `
                   -ldflags "-s -w -X main.CompileVersion=$version" `
                   -o "dist/$bin"

      - name: Upload portable artifact
        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        with:
          name: bin-${{ matrix.goos }}-${{ matrix.goarch }}
          path: dist/

# ---------- DUCKDB: Linux/amd64 (glibc 2.28) в контейнере + macOS (amd64/arm64) ----------
  build_duckdb:
    if: contains(toJson(github.event.head_commit.message), 'stable release') || github.event_name == 'workflow_dispatch'
    runs-on: ${{ matrix.runner }}
    strategy:
      fail-fast: false
      matrix:
        include:
          # macOS amd64/arm64: нативно
          - { target: darwin_amd64_duckdb, goos: darwin, goarch: amd64, goversion: "1.23", runner: "macos-13", in_container: false }
          - { target: darwin_arm64_duckdb, goos: darwin, goarch: arm64, goversion: "1.23", runner: "macos-14", in_container: false }

    steps:
      - name: Checkout
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      # --- macOS нативно ---
      - name: Set up Go (macOS)
        if: matrix.in_container == false
        uses: actions/setup-go@v5
        with:
          go-version: "${{ matrix.goversion }}"
          cache: true
          cache-dependency-path: go.sum

      - name: go mod tidy (macOS)
        if: matrix.in_container == false
        shell: bash
        run: |
          go get github.com/marcboeker/go-duckdb/v2@latest
          go mod tidy

      - name: Build (macOS duckdb)
        if: matrix.in_container == false
        shell: bash
        env:
          GOOS: ${{ matrix.goos }}
          GOARCH: ${{ matrix.goarch }}
        run: |
          set -euo pipefail
          export CGO_ENABLED=1
          VERSION=${{ github.run_number }}
          mkdir -p dist
          BIN="chicha-isotope-map_${GOOS}_${GOARCH}_duckdb"
          GOFLAGS="-trimpath -buildvcs=false" \
          go build -tags "netgo,osusergo,duckdb" \
                   -ldflags "-s -w -X main.CompileVersion=$VERSION" \
                   -o "dist/${BIN}"

      - name: Upload duckdb artifact
        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        with:
          name: bin-${{ matrix.target }}
          path: dist/

  release:
    needs: [build_portable, build_duckdb]
    runs-on: ubuntu-24.04
    steps:
      - name: Download all artifacts
        uses: actions/download-artifact@v4
        with:
          path: dist-raw/

      - name: Publish GitHub Release (tag latest)
        uses: softprops/action-gh-release@v2
        env:
          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
        with:
          tag_name: latest
          name: "download stable version"
          prerelease: false
          files: dist-raw/**/*
name: Test

on:
  push:
    branches: [ main ]
  pull_request:
    branches: [ main ]
  workflow_dispatch:

jobs:
  test:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest

    steps:
      - name: Checkout
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Set up Go
        uses: actions/setup-go@v5
        with:
          go-version: "1.23"
          cache: true
          cache-dependency-path: go.sum

      - name: Run tests
        run: go test ./pkg/...
```

id: toolu_01TAEcjv6rbY7XeVxMdvLwa7
```
Perfect! Now I have enough information to provide a comprehensive assessment. Let me compile my findings:

## Merge Feasibility Assessment: safecast-new-map + safecast-map-MCP

### 1. Repository Overview

**safecast-new-map (Map Server)**
- **Module**: `safecast-new-map`
- **Go Version**: 1.24
- **Main Entry**: `safecast-new-map.go` (~32,844 lines of Go code)
- **Structure**: Single monolithic binary with embedded web UI
- **Dependencies**: PostgreSQL (pgx), SQLite (modernc), DuckDB, msgpack, crypto
- **Binaries**: `safecast-new-map` (the map web server)

**safecast-map-MCP (MCP Server + Web Chat)**
- **Module**: `github.com/your-org/safecast-mcp-server` (in `/go` subdirectory)
- **Go Version**: 1.25.7
- **Entry Points**: 
  - `/go/cmd/mcp-server/main.go` (~7,392 lines total)
  - `/go/cmd/web-chat/main.go` (additional CLI tool)
- **Structure**: Multiple binaries under `/go/cmd/` directory
- **Dependencies**: MCP protocol (mark3labs/mcp-go), Swagger/API docs, PostgreSQL, DuckDB
- **Binaries**: `safecast-mcp`, `safecast-web-chat`

---

### 2. Directory Structure Comparison

```
safecast-new-map/
├── pkg/
│   ├── api/
│   ├── auth/
│   ├── database/          # Database models & utilities (142KB+)
│   ├── email/
│   ├── web/               # Web server code
│   ├── safecast-fetcher/
│   ├── safecast-realtime/
│   ├── ...
├── public_html/           # Embedded web UI (JS, images, GeoJSON)
├── migrations/            # 7 SQL migrations
├── safecast-new-map.go    # Single main file

safecast-map-MCP/
├── go/
│   ├── cmd/
│   │   ├── mcp-server/    # 34 .go files (tools, REST endpoints, main)
│   │   └── web-chat/      # Web chat CLI
│   ├── go.mod             # Separate module at /go level
│   └── go.sum
├── js/                    # Empty (development area)
├── go.mod                 # Root placeholder (trivial)
└── docs/                  # Architecture documentation
```

---

### 3. Key Conflicts & Issues

#### A. **Module Path Conflict**
- safecast-new-map: `module safecast-new-map`
- safecast-map-MCP/go: `module github.com/your-org/safecast-mcp-server`
- **Risk**: Module name needs to be corrected (placeholder module name in MCP)

#### B. **Separate go.mod Files**
- safecast-map-MCP has `/go/go.mod` (the real one) and root `/go.mod` (placeholder)
- Merging requires consolidating to single go.mod at monorepo root or managing workspace

#### C. **Go Version Mismatch**
- safecast-new-map: Go 1.24
- safecast-map-MCP: Go 1.25.7
- **Needs standardization** to Go 1.25.7

#### D. **Deployment & Service Names**
- safecast-new-map deploys to: `/usr/local/bin/safecast-new-map` (single service)
- safecast-map-MCP deploys to: `/root/safecast-mcp-server/safecast-mcp` and `/root/safecast-web-chat-server/safecast-web-chat` (separate services)
- **Risk**: Different deployment paths and systemd service names would conflict if merged into one build

#### E. **Different Build Outputs**
- safecast-new-map: Single binary with embedded assets
- safecast-map-MCP: Multiple independent binaries (mcp-server, web-chat)
- **Merge approach needed**: Either multi-binary monorepo or consolidate to single binary

---

### 4. Shared Dependencies & Code

**Overlapping Dependencies:**
- PostgreSQL via `pgx/v5` (both projects)
- DuckDB (both projects)
- Google UUID (both projects)
- Compression libraries (arrow-go, lz4, etc.)

**No Direct Code Sharing:**
- Database layer: safecast-new-map has comprehensive `/pkg/database/` module; MCP server calls it via REST API (`defaultBaseURL = "https://simplemap.safecast.org"`)
- Web UI: safecast-new-map has embedded UI; MCP server has REST endpoints only
- Authentication: safecast-new-map handles user auth; MCP server is read-only

**Current Integration:**
- MCP server is a **client** of the map server (REST API calls)
- Not co-located; MCP calls `https://simplemap.safecast.org/api/*` endpoints
- This suggests they're **intentionally separate** for architectural reasons

---

### 5. GitHub Actions Workflows

**safecast-new-map:**
- Single deploy workflow: builds → deploys `/usr/local/bin/safecast-new-map` → stops/starts `safecast-new-map` service
- Release workflow: multi-platform portable builds (Linux, macOS, Windows, BSD)
- Invalidates CloudFront cache post-deploy

**safecast-map-MCP:**
- Build both binaries from `/go/cmd/` subdirectories
- Deploys both `safecast-mcp` and `safecast-web-chat` as separate services
- More complex: sets up systemd services, nginx reloads, health checks
- Environment-specific .env files for each service

**Merge Impact**: Workflows would need significant refactoring to handle multiple binaries

---

### 6. Shared Infrastructure (Database)

- Both connect to same **PostgreSQL database** (`safecast` database)
- Both use same `realtime_measurements`, `tracks`, `spectra` tables
- **Migrations**: safecast-new-map has 7 migrations; safecast-map-MCP has 1
- **Risk**: If merged, migrations need coordination to avoid conflicts

---

### 7. Merge Feasibility: Challenges & Risks

| Challenge | Severity | Notes |
|-----------|----------|-------|
| **Monolithic vs. Modular** | HIGH | safecast-new-map is single binary; MCP is multi-binary. Architecture mismatch. |
| **Module naming** | MEDIUM | MCP module is placeholder (`github.com/your-org/...`). Needs proper naming. |
| **Deployment separation** | HIGH | Two projects deploy to entirely different paths/services. Merging would require rewriting deployment logic. |
| **API coupling** | MEDIUM | MCP intentionally calls map server via REST. Deep merge would break this abstraction. |
| **Go version** | LOW | Easy to standardize on 1.25.7 |
| **Build complexity** | MEDIUM | Single go.mod build would need to produce 2-3 independent binaries. Requires careful structuring. |
| **Systemd services** | MEDIUM | Two separate services; merged repo would need dual-service deploy logic. |
| **Migration management** | LOW | Small number of migrations; can be coordinated. |

---

### 8. Recommended Merge Strategy (if proceeding)

If you decide to merge, here's the optimal approach:

```
safecast-monorepo/
├── go.mod                    # Single root module (Go 1.25.7)
├── cmd/
│   ├── map-server/          # Moved from safecast-new-map/
│   │   └── main.go
│   ├── mcp-server/          # From safecast-map-MCP/go/cmd/mcp-server/
│   └── web-chat/            # From safecast-map-MCP/go/cmd/web-chat/
├── pkg/                      # Shared code
│   ├── api/
│   ├── auth/
│   ├── database/
│   ├── mcp/                 # New: shared MCP utilities
│   └── ...
├── migrations/              # Combined database migrations
├── public_html/             # Map server web assets
├── .github/workflows/
│   ├── deploy-all.yml       # Build all binaries, deploy all
│   └── release.yml          # Multi-platform releases
└── docs/                    # Combined documentation
```

**Benefits:**
- Single dependency tree
- Shared database utilities
- Unified CI/CD
- Easier refactoring of REST API abstraction

**Drawbacks:**
- More complex build process (3 binaries)
- Risk of coupling map-server and mcp-server more tightly
- More complex deployment workflow
- Requires careful package organization to avoid circular imports

---

### 9. Alternative: Keep Separate but Better Organized

**This might be better** if:
- You want to maintain architectural separation (API boundary between services)
- Different release cycles are important
- Team is split across services

The current structure actually **follows microservices principles well**—keeping the services in separate repos allows independent scaling, development, and deployment.

---

## Summary

**Can they be merged? Yes, but with significant complexity.**

**Should they be merged? Questionable.**

The repositories serve different purposes:
- **safecast-new-map**: Web server + database + UI
- **safecast-map-MCP**: AI-facing protocol layer + chat UI

They're currently **intentionally decoupled** (MCP calls map-server via REST API). Merging would require:
1. Rewriting the build system to produce multiple binaries
2. Consolidating deployment workflows
3. Handling separate systemd services in unified deploy
4. Refactoring the REST API abstraction they currently maintain

**Recommendation**: Unless there's a strong business reason (shared development team, unified releases, code deduplication), keep them separate. The microservices architecture they have now is cleaner and more maintainable.

**Yes, technically possible — but it's more complex than it's worth.**

### Main challenges

| **Multiple binaries** | MCP repo builds 2 binaries (`safecast-mcp`, `safecast-web-chat`); map builds 1. A merged repo needs to produce all 3. |
| **Deployment** | They deploy to different paths and run as different systemd services. Workflows need significant rewriting. |

### The real question

They're **intentionally decoupled** right now — the MCP server calls the map server via REST API (`simplemap.safecast.org/api/*`). Merging them into one repo doesn't change that architecture; they'd still run as separate processes on the server.

**Merging makes sense if** you want a single place to manage all code, unified CI/CD, and shared Go packages (especially the database layer, which is currently duplicated).

**Staying separate makes sense if** you want independent deployment cycles and the architectural boundary between the map API and the MCP layer to remain explicit.

What's your main motivation for merging — simpler maintenance, shared code, or something else?

```go
// Static assets
http.Handle("/static/", http.StripPrefix("/static/", http.FileServer(http.FS(staticFS))))
http.Handle("/js/", http.StripPrefix("/js/", http.FileServer(http.Dir("public_html/"))))

// Core routes
http.HandleFunc("/home", homeHandler)
http.HandleFunc("/", mapHandler)

// Authentication routes (conditional on authManager)
http.HandleFunc("/api/auth/register", authManager.RegisterHandler)
http.HandleFunc("/api/auth/login", authManager.LoginHandler)
http.HandleFunc("/api/auth/logout", authManager.LogoutHandler)
http.HandleFunc("/api/auth/forgot-password", authManager.ForgotPasswordHandler)
http.HandleFunc("/api/auth/reset-password", authManager.ResetPasswordHandler)
http.HandleFunc("/api/auth/verify-email", authManager.VerifyEmailHandler)
http.HandleFunc("/api/user/profile", authManager.RequireAuth(...))
http.HandleFunc("/api/user/change-password", authManager.RequireAuth(...))
http.HandleFunc("/api/user/uploads", authManager.RequireAuth(...))

// Admin routes (wrapped with OptionalAuth)
http.HandleFunc("/admin/users", authManager.OptionalAuth(...))
http.HandleFunc("/admin/uploads", authManager.OptionalAuth(...))
http.HandleFunc("/api/admin/users", authManager.OptionalAuth(...))
http.HandleFunc("/api/admin/users/create", authManager.OptionalAuth(...))
http.HandleFunc("/api/admin/users/", authManager.OptionalAuth(...))  // PUT/PATCH/DELETE/POST

// Uploads & markers
http.HandleFunc("/upload", uploadHandler)              // Protected if requireAuth=true
http.HandleFunc("/upload/progress", progressHandler)
http.HandleFunc("/get_markers", getMarkersHandler)
http.HandleFunc("/stream_markers", streamMarkersHandler)  // SSE streaming
http.HandleFunc("/realtime_history", realtimeHistoryHandler)

// Track routes
http.HandleFunc("/trackid/", trackHandler)
http.HandleFunc("/tracks/", tracksHandler)

// Admin API endpoints (conditional on authManager)
http.HandleFunc("/api/admin/uploads", adminUploadsHandler)
http.HandleFunc("/api/admin/tracks", adminTracksHandler)
http.HandleFunc("/api/admin/backfill", adminBackfillHandler)
http.HandleFunc("/api/admin/backfill-countries", adminBackfillCountriesHandler)
http.HandleFunc("/api/admin/delete", adminDeleteTrackHandler)
http.HandleFunc("/api/admin/delete-multiple", adminDeleteMultipleTracksHandler)
http.HandleFunc("/api/admin/import-from-safecast", adminImportFromSafecastHandler)
http.HandleFunc("/api/admin/cache", adminCacheHandler)
```

---

### Key Architecture Notes for Merge Planning

1. **Single Binary Monolith**: All functionality compiled into one executable
2. **Multi-Database Support**: Abstracted via `pkg/database` (PostgreSQL, SQLite, DuckDB)
3. **Embedded Assets**: Static files and licenses embedded via `//go:embed`
4. **Middleware Pattern**: `authManager.RequireAuth()`, `OptionalAuth()`, `importShield()` wrapping handlers
5. **Stream Support**: Server-Sent Events (SSE) for `/stream_markers` without gzip compression
6. **API Handler Registration**: Two-step process - base routes registered directly, API routes via `apiHandler.Register()`
7. **Background Services**: Async indexing, selfupgrade, safecast-fetcher, safecast-realtime
8. **Conditional Features**: Auth system, admin panel, safecast integration all optional based on flags

### 3. WEB-CHAT MAIN.GO (First 50 Lines)

**File**: `/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/safecast-map-MCP/go/cmd/web-chat/main.go`

```go
package main

import (
	"bytes"
	"context"
	_ "embed"
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"log"
	"net/http"
	"os"

	mcpclient "github.com/mark3labs/mcp-go/client"
	"github.com/mark3labs/mcp-go/mcp"
)

//go:embed index.html
var indexHTML []byte

//go:embed safecast-square-ct.png
var logoPNG []byte

const systemPrompt = `Safecast radiation monitoring assistant with REAL-TIME sensor data and historical archives.

**Tool Selection**
- Current/live data: sensor_current (returns actual CPM readings), list_sensors (metadata/discovery only)
- Time-series from fixed sensors: sensor_history
- Extreme readings with locations: query_extreme_readings
- Statistics: radiation_stats
- Historical surveys: query_radiation, search_area, list_tracks
- NEVER use query_radiation for current data (historical only)
- NEVER use radiation_stats for specific extreme location queries
- NEVER use list_sensors when the user wants radiation readings — use sensor_current instead

**Device type names** (exact values in the database):
- bGeigieZen → "geigiecast-zen" (IDs like geigiecast-zen:65002)
- bGeigie → "geigiecast" (IDs like geigiecast:62007) — MOBILE only
- Pointcast → "pointcast" (IDs like pointcast:10042)
- Solarcast → "solarcast"
- Notehub/Radnote/Blues → "notehub" (IDs like note:dev:867648049123019)
- nGeigie → "ngeigie" (IDs like ngeigie:101)
- Direct TCP → "device-tcp" (IDs like safecast:3474557222)

**Data Types**
- Real-time fixed stations: geigiecast-zen, pointcast, solarcast, notehub, ngeigie, device-tcp → sensor_current or sensor_history
- Mobile surveys only: geigiecast → query_radiation, list_tracks, device_history
- CPM → µSv/h: multiply by ~0.0069 (LND 7318)
- NEVER use device_history for any fixed sensor type — use sensor_current instead
```

### 4. GITHUB ACTIONS WORKFLOW

**File**: `/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/safecast-map-MCP/.github/workflows/deploy.yml`

Complete workflow (165 lines) - **Summary**:

**Build Stage**:
- Cross-compile for Linux x86_64 (Go 1.25.7)
- Builds: `safecast-mcp` (mcp-server) and `safecast-web-chat` (web-chat)
- Triggers on: `push` to `main` (paths: go/cmd/**, go/go.mod, go/go.sum, deploy.yml)

**Deploy Stage**:
- SSH to `MAP_SERVER_HOST` (65.108.24.131) using `SSH_PRIVATE_KEY` secret
- Deploys to `/root/safecast-mcp-server/` and `/root/safecast-web-chat-server/`
- Creates `.env` files for both services:
  - **MCP**: `MCP_BASE_URL=https://simplemap.safecast.org/mcp`, `DATABASE_URL=postgres://...`, `DUCKDB_PATH=/root/safecast-mcp-server/analytics.duckdb`
  - **Web-Chat**: `MCP_URL=http://localhost:3333/mcp-http`, `PORT=3334`, `CLAUDE_MODEL=claude-haiku-4-5-20251001`, `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY=...`

**Systemd Management**:
- Stops service before binary replacement
- Creates/updates systemd service file for `safecast-web-chat` (idempotent)
- Service dependencies: `safecast-web-chat` wants `safecast-mcp.service`

**Health Checks**:
- MCP: POST to `https://simplemap.safecast.org/mcp-http` (MCP initialize call)
- Web-Chat: SSH curl to `http://localhost:3334/health` (retries 5x, non-blocking failure)

---

### 5. WEB-CHAT FUNCTIONALITY SUMMARY

**Purpose**: Claude AI assistant web interface for Safecast radiation monitoring

**What it Does**:
1. Embeds `index.html` and `safecast-square-ct.png` logo as Go binary assets
2. Acts as an MCP client connecting to the mcp-server (default: `http://localhost:3333/mcp-http`)
3. Proxies user questions to Claude API (claude-haiku-4-5-20251001) with MCP tools available
4. Provides a dark-themed chat UI (HTML+JS embedded in binary) for radiation data queries
5. Implements system prompt guiding Claude on proper tool selection for radiation queries
6. Serves on port 3334 (configurable via `PORT` env var)

**HTTP Endpoints**:
- `/health` - Health check (returns 200 OK)
- `/` - Serves chat UI (index.html)
- (Likely POST /chat or similar for message handling)

WorkingDirectory=/root/safecast-mcp-server
ExecStart=/root/safecast-mcp-server/safecast-mcp

RestartSec=10
StandardOutput=journal
StandardError=journal
EnvironmentFile=/root/safecast-mcp-server/.env

Description=Safecast Web Chat (MCP + Claude)
After=network.target safecast-mcp.service
Wants=safecast-mcp.service

[Service]
Type=simple
User=root
WorkingDirectory=/root/safecast-web-chat-server
ExecStart=/root/safecast-web-chat-server/safecast-web-chat
Restart=always
RestartSec=10
StandardOutput=journal
StandardError=journal
EnvironmentFile=/root/safecast-web-chat-server/.env
NoNewPrivileges=true
```

## Approved Plan:
# Plan: Merge safecast-map-MCP into safecast-new-map

## Context
The MCP server and web-chat assistant live in a separate repo (safecast-map-MCP) but deploy to the same VPS as the map server. The user wants simpler maintenance: one repo, one deployment pipeline, one place to track progress. After the merge, the MCP repo will be archived on GitHub.

## What gets merged
Three things come from `safecast-map-MCP/go/`:
1. `cmd/mcp-server/` — ~35 .go files, the MCP+REST API server (port 3333)
2. `cmd/web-chat/` — Claude AI chat UI (port 3334)
3. `go/migrations/add_detector_index.sql` → `migrations/`

## Target structure in safecast-new-map
```
safecast-new-map/
├── cmd/
│   ├── mcp-server/       ← moved from safecast-map-MCP/go/cmd/mcp-server/
│   └── web-chat/         ← moved from safecast-map-MCP/go/cmd/web-chat/
├── pkg/                  ← unchanged
├── safecast-new-map.go   ← unchanged (stays as root main package)
├── go.mod                ← merge MCP dependencies in
└── .github/workflows/
    ├── deploy.yml        ← extend to also build+deploy mcp+webchat
    ├── test.yml          ← unchanged
    └── stable-release.yml ← unchanged
```

## Step-by-step implementation

### 1. Copy MCP source files
```
cp -r safecast-map-MCP/go/cmd/mcp-server  safecast-new-map/cmd/mcp-server
cp -r safecast-map-MCP/go/cmd/web-chat    safecast-new-map/cmd/web-chat
cp safecast-map-MCP/go/migrations/add_detector_index.sql  safecast-new-map/migrations/
```

### 2. Fix module import path in mcp-server
In `cmd/mcp-server/rest.go` line 32, change:
```go
_ "github.com/your-org/safecast-mcp-server/cmd/mcp-server/docs"
```
to:
```go
_ "safecast-new-map/cmd/mcp-server/docs"
```
And in `cmd/mcp-server/docs/docs.go`, the `SwaggerInfo` init block — no changes needed there (it's generated code, no import of the parent module).

### 3. Merge go.mod
Add MCP-only dependencies to safecast-new-map/go.mod:
- `github.com/mark3labs/mcp-go` (MCP protocol)
- `github.com/swaggo/http-swagger` (Swagger UI handler)
- `github.com/swaggo/swag` (Swagger codegen — for `swag init` regeneration)
- `github.com/swaggo/files`
- Other transitive deps that don't already exist

Both repos already share: `pgx/v5`, `go-duckdb`, `google/uuid` — no conflict.

Bump Go version in go.mod from `1.24` to `1.24` (keep as-is; MCP's `1.25.7` is a toolchain directive not a hard requirement — we can update later if needed).

Run `go mod tidy` to reconcile.

### 4. Update GitHub Actions deploy.yml
Extend the existing workflow to also build and deploy the two MCP binaries.

Add build steps:
```yaml
- name: Build MCP server
  run: go build -o safecast-mcp ./cmd/mcp-server/

- name: Build web-chat
  run: go build -o safecast-web-chat ./cmd/web-chat/
```

Add deploy steps (after existing map server deploy):
```yaml
- name: Deploy MCP server
  run: |
    ssh ... "systemctl stop safecast-mcp || true"
    rsync safecast-mcp root@65.108.24.131:/root/safecast-mcp-server/safecast-mcp
    ssh ... "systemctl start safecast-mcp"

- name: Deploy web-chat
  run: |
    ssh ... "systemctl stop safecast-web-chat || true"
    rsync safecast-web-chat root@65.108.24.131:/root/safecast-web-chat-server/safecast-web-chat
    ssh ... "systemctl start safecast-web-chat"
```

Add required secrets to the map repo (they're already in MCP repo):
- `DATABASE_URL` — postgres connection string for MCP
- `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` — for web-chat Claude calls
- (SSH key already exists as `DEPLOY_SSH_KEY`, just needs alias)

### 5. Verify build locally
```bash
go build -o safecast-new-map .
go build -o safecast-mcp ./cmd/mcp-server/
go build -o safecast-web-chat ./cmd/web-chat/
go test ./pkg/...
```

### 6. Commit, push, verify CI
- All 3 binaries build in CI
- All 3 services restarted on VPS
- Check: https://simplemap.safecast.org/docs/ still works
- Check: https://simplemap.safecast.org/assistant/ still works

### 7. Archive MCP repo on GitHub
Via GitHub UI or `gh repo archive Safecast/safecast-map-MCP`

## Critical files to modify
- `safecast-new-map/go.mod` — add MCP deps
- `safecast-new-map/cmd/mcp-server/rest.go:32` — fix import path
- `safecast-new-map/.github/workflows/deploy.yml` — add MCP+webchat build/deploy steps

## Secrets to add to safecast-new-map repo
- `DATABASE_URL` (MCP uses direct DB connection)
- `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` (web-chat)

## Risks
- `go mod tidy` may pull in conflicting dep versions — resolve case by case
- The `swag init` generated `docs/docs.go` references the old module path in `SwaggerInfo.BasePath` — this is runtime data, not an import, so it's fine as-is

```

> AGENT

The problem is clear: when we added `/api/` → port 3333 to the nginx origin block to fix `/docs/`, it broke ALL `/api/auth/*`, `/api/user/*`, `/api/admin/*` routes that need to go to the map server on port 8765.

Need to add specific rules for the map server's auth/user/admin paths *before* the general `/api/` rule:

> TOOL

tool_use Bash
id: toolu_01NhdMTkUdX8oTj1KB7J1kaG
```json
{
  "command": "ssh -i ~/.ssh/safecast-deploy root@65.108.24.131 \"cat /etc/nginx/sites-available/origin-simplemap.safecast.org\"",
  "description": "Read current origin-simplemap nginx config"
}
```

> TOOL

tool_result
id: toolu_01NhdMTkUdX8oTj1KB7J1kaG
```
Ubuntu 24.04.4 LTS
server {
    listen 443 ssl http2;
    listen [::]:443 ssl http2;
    server_name origin-simplemap.safecast.org;

    ssl_certificate /etc/letsencrypt/live/simplemap.safecast.org/fullchain.pem;
    ssl_certificate_key /etc/letsencrypt/live/simplemap.safecast.org/privkey.pem;
    include /etc/letsencrypt/options-ssl-nginx.conf;
    ssl_dhparam /etc/letsencrypt/ssl-dhparams.pem;

    # MCP Server SSE transport
    location /mcp {
        proxy_pass http://localhost:3333;
        proxy_http_version 1.1;
        proxy_set_header Upgrade $http_upgrade;
        proxy_set_header Connection "upgrade";
        proxy_set_header Host $host;
        proxy_set_header X-Real-IP $remote_addr;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto $scheme;
        proxy_read_timeout 86400;
    }

    # MCP Server HTTP transport (used by Claude.ai and other MCP clients)
    location /mcp-http {
        proxy_pass http://localhost:3333;
        proxy_http_version 1.1;
        proxy_set_header Host $host;
        proxy_set_header X-Real-IP $remote_addr;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto $scheme;
    }

    # Safecast Web Chat (MCP + Claude streaming)
    location /assistant/ {
        proxy_pass http://localhost:3334/;
        proxy_http_version 1.1;
        proxy_set_header Host $host;
        proxy_set_header X-Real-IP $remote_addr;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto $scheme;
        proxy_buffering off;
        proxy_read_timeout 120s;
        proxy_cache off;
    }

    # MCP REST API documentation
    location /docs/ {
        proxy_pass http://localhost:3333/docs/;
        proxy_set_header Host $host;
        proxy_set_header X-Real-IP $remote_addr;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto $scheme;
    }

    # MCP REST API endpoints
    location /api/ {
        proxy_read_timeout 120;
        proxy_pass http://localhost:3333/api/;
        proxy_set_header Host $host;
        proxy_set_header X-Real-IP $remote_addr;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto $scheme;
    }

    # Everything else (map app, /static/, etc.) goes to the map server
    location / {
        proxy_pass http://localhost:8765;
        proxy_http_version 1.1;
        proxy_set_header Upgrade $http_upgrade;
        proxy_set_header Connection "upgrade";
        proxy_set_header Host $host;
        proxy_set_header X-Real-IP $remote_addr;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto $scheme;
        client_max_body_size 100M;
    }
}
```

  "description": "Insert auth/user/admin location blocks before /api/ using Python"
}

> AGENT

Invalidation is in progress. Two issues found and fixed:

1. **Root cause**: When we fixed `/docs/` this morning, we added `location /api/` → MCP server (port 3333) to the origin nginx block. This accidentally routed ALL `/api/*` to MCP, breaking auth.

2. **Fix**: Added specific `location /api/auth/`, `/api/user/`, `/api/admin/` blocks that route to the map server (port 8765) *before* the general `/api/` rule. CloudFront cache invalidated.

The reset password should work again within ~30 seconds as CloudFront propagates. Try again shortly!

Same issue — `/api/spectrum/` also goes to MCP. Rather than whack-a-mole, let me fix this properly by routing only the specific MCP endpoints to port 3333 and sending everything else to 8765:

> TOOL

tool_use Bash
id: toolu_0133QgrsWB54156z6a6rM6Wm
```json
{
  "command": "ssh -i ~/.ssh/safecast-deploy root@65.108.24.131 \"python3 -c \\\"\ncontent = open('/etc/nginx/sites-available/origin-simplemap.safecast.org').read()\n\n# Replace the broad /api/ → 3333 rule with specific MCP-only endpoints\nold = '''    # MCP REST API endpoints\n    location /api/ {\n        proxy_read_timeout 120;\n        proxy_pass http://localhost:3333/api/;\n        proxy_set_header Host \\\\\\$host;\n        proxy_set_header X-Real-IP \\\\\\$remote_addr;\n        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For \\\\\\$proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;\n        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto \\\\\\$scheme;\n    }'''\n\nnew = '''    # MCP REST API — only route MCP-specific endpoints to port 3333\n    location = /api/radiation  { proxy_read_timeout 120; proxy_pass http://localhost:3333/api/radiation; proxy_set_header Host \\\\\\$host; proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto \\\\\\$scheme; }\n    location = /api/area       { proxy_read_timeout 120; proxy_pass http://localhost:3333/api/area; proxy_set_header Host \\\\\\$host; proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto \\\\\\$scheme; }\n    location = /api/sensors    { proxy_read_timeout 120; proxy_pass http://localhost:3333/api/sensors; proxy_set_header Host \\\\\\$host; proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto \\\\\\$scheme; }\n    location /api/sensor/      { proxy_read_timeout 120; proxy_pass http://localhost:3333/api/sensor/; proxy_set_header Host \\\\\\$host; proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto \\\\\\$scheme; }\n    location /api/device/      { proxy_read_timeout 120; proxy_pass http://localhost:3333/api/device/; proxy_set_header Host \\\\\\$host; proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto \\\\\\$scheme; }\n    location = /api/spectra    { proxy_read_timeout 120; proxy_pass http://localhost:3333/api/spectra; proxy_set_header Host \\\\\\$host; proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto \\\\\\$scheme; }\n    location = /api/stats      { proxy_read_timeout 120; proxy_pass http://localhost:3333/api/stats; proxy_set_header Host \\\\\\$host; proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto \\\\\\$scheme; }\n    location = /api/extreme    { proxy_read_timeout 120; proxy_pass http://localhost:3333/api/extreme; proxy_set_header Host \\\\\\$host; proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto \\\\\\$scheme; }\n    location /api/info/        { proxy_read_timeout 120; proxy_pass http://localhost:3333/api/info/; proxy_set_header Host \\\\\\$host; proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto \\\\\\$scheme; }\n    location /api/gpt/         { proxy_read_timeout 120; proxy_pass http://localhost:3333/api/gpt/; proxy_set_header Host \\\\\\$host; proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto \\\\\\$scheme; }\n    location /api/track/       { proxy_read_timeout 120; proxy_pass http://localhost:3333/api/track/; proxy_set_header Host \\\\\\$host; proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto \\\\\\$scheme; }'''\n\ncontent = content.replace(old, new)\nopen('/etc/nginx/sites-available/origin-simplemap.safecast.org', 'w').write(content)\nprint('done')\n\\\"\"",
  "description": "Replace broad /api/ rule with specific MCP endpoint routes"
}

    10→User Request
    11→    ↓
    12→CloudFront (simplemap.safecast.org)
    13→    ↓ HTTPS (port 443)
    14→origin-simplemap.safecast.org
    15→    ↓
    16→Nginx (routes by path)
    17→    ├─ /mcp-http → MCP Server (port 3333)
    18→    ├─ /mcp      → MCP Server (port 3333)
    19→    ├─ /docs/    → MCP Server (port 3333)
    20→    ├─ /api/mcp/ → MCP Server (port 3333)
    21→    └─ /         → Map Server (port 8765)
    22→```
    23→
    24→## Key Configuration
    25→
    26→### 1. SSL Certificate
    27→
    28→The Let's Encrypt certificate includes both domains:
    29→```
    30→DNS:simplemap.safecast.org
    31→DNS:origin-simplemap.safecast.org
    32→```
    33→
    34→Renew/expand certificate:
    35→```bash
    36→certbot certonly --nginx \
    37→  -d simplemap.safecast.org \
    38→  -d origin-simplemap.safecast.org \
    39→  --expand
    40→```
    41→
    42→### 2. Nginx Configuration
    43→
    44→**Server names:** `/etc/nginx/sites-enabled/simplemap.safecast.org`
    45→```nginx
    46→server {
    47→    server_name simplemap.safecast.org origin-simplemap.safecast.org;
    48→
    49→    # MCP Server endpoints
    50→    location /mcp-http {
    51→        proxy_pass http://localhost:3333;
    52→        proxy_http_version 1.1;
    53→        proxy_set_header Host $host;
    54→        proxy_set_header X-Real-IP $remote_addr;
    55→        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;
    56→        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto $scheme;
    57→    }
    58→
    59→    location /mcp {
    60→        proxy_pass http://localhost:3333;
    61→        proxy_http_version 1.1;
    62→        proxy_set_header Upgrade $http_upgrade;
    63→        proxy_set_header Connection "upgrade";
    64→        # ... other headers
    65→    }
    66→
    67→    location /docs/ {
    68→        proxy_pass http://localhost:3333/docs/;
    69→        # ... headers
    70→    }
    71→
    72→    location /api/mcp/ {
    73→        proxy_pass http://localhost:3333/api/;
    74→        # ... headers
    75→    }
    76→
    77→    # Map server (catch-all)
    78→    location / {
    79→        proxy_pass http://localhost:8765;
    80→        # ... headers
    81→    }
    82→
    83→    listen [::]:443 ssl ipv6only=on;
    84→    listen 443 ssl;
    85→    ssl_certificate /etc/letsencrypt/live/simplemap.safecast.org/fullchain.pem;
    86→    ssl_certificate_key /etc/letsencrypt/live/simplemap.safecast.org/privkey.pem;
    87→}
    88→```
    89→
    90→### 3. CloudFront Distribution
    91→
    92→**Distribution ID:** E12FYIQ8RRXOJ1
    93→
    94→**Origin Settings:**
    95→- Origin Domain: `origin-simplemap.safecast.org`
    96→- Protocol: HTTPS only
    97→- HTTPS Port: 443
    98→- HTTP Port: 80
    99→- Origin Protocol Policy: `https-only`
   100→- Custom Headers: None (uses default Host header)
   101→
   102→**Cache Behavior:**
   103→- Allowed Methods: GET, HEAD, OPTIONS, PUT, POST, PATCH, DELETE
   104→- Viewer Protocol Policy: Redirect HTTP to HTTPS
   105→- Origin Request Policy: AllViewer
   106→- Compress: Yes
   107→
   108→**Domain:**
   109→- CNAME: `simplemap.safecast.org`
   110→- SSL Certificate: ACM certificate in us-east-1
   111→
   112→## Testing
   113→
   114→Test the setup:
   115→```bash
   116→# MCP Server
   117→curl -X POST https://simplemap.safecast.org/mcp-http \
   118→  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
   119→  -d '{"jsonrpc":"2.0","id":1,"method":"initialize","params":{"protocolVersion":"2024-11-05","capabilities":{},"clientInfo":{"name":"test","version":"1.0"}}}'
   120→
   121→# Map Page
   122→curl -I https://simplemap.safecast.org/
   123→
   124→# Map API
   125→curl https://simplemap.safecast.org/api/tracks?limit=1
   126→```
   127→
   128→## Connecting to MCP Server
   129→
   130→### Claude Code CLI
   131→```bash
   132→claude mcp add --transport http safecast https://simplemap.safecast.org/mcp-http
   133→```
   134→
   135→### Claude.ai Web Interface
   136→1. Settings → Integrations → Add custom integration
   137→2. URL: `https://simplemap.safecast.org/mcp-http`
   138→
   139→## Troubleshooting
   140→
   141→### CloudFront returns 502 errors
   142→**Cause:** SSL certificate doesn't include the origin domain
   143→
   144→**Fix:** Ensure certificate includes both domains:
   145→```bash
   146→certbot certonly --nginx \
   147→  -d simplemap.safecast.org \
   148→  -d origin-simplemap.safecast.org \
   149→  --expand
   150→```
   151→
   152→### MCP requests go to map server
   153→**Cause:** Nginx not matching the correct server_name
   154→
   155→**Fix:** Check nginx server_name includes both domains:
   156→```bash
   157→grep server_name /etc/nginx/sites-enabled/simplemap.safecast.org
   158→# Should show: server_name simplemap.safecast.org origin-simplemap.safecast.org;
   159→```
   160→
   161→### Certificate renewal fails
   162→**Cause:** CloudFront is intercepting Let's Encrypt validation
   163→
   164→**Solution:** Temporarily change CloudFront origin to port 8765 (direct to map server):
   165→```bash
   166→# Update CloudFront
   167→aws cloudfront get-distribution-config --id E12FYIQ8RRXOJ1 > /tmp/cf.json
   168→# Edit: CustomOriginConfig.HTTPPort = 8765, OriginProtocolPolicy = "http-only"
   169→# Apply changes, wait for deployment
   170→# Run certbot
   171→# Revert CloudFront back to port 443, https-only
   172→```
   173→
   174→## Important Notes
   175→
   176→- **DNS Records:**
   177→  - `simplemap.safecast.org` → CloudFront (ALIAS record)
   178→  - `origin-simplemap.safecast.org` → 65.108.24.131 (A record)
   179→
   180→- **SSH/Rsync:** Always use IP address (65.108.24.131), not domain name
   181→
   182→- **CloudFront Invalidation:** After deploying new code:
   183→  ```bash
   184→  aws cloudfront create-invalidation --distribution-id E12FYIQ8RRXOJ1 --paths "/*"
   185→  ```
   186→
   187→- **Swagger Documentation:** The MCP server's Swagger docs at `/docs/` are configured with:
   188→  - Host: `simplemap.safecast.org`
   189→  - Base Path: `/api/mcp`
   190→
   191→  If these values need updating, edit `go/cmd/mcp-server/rest.go` annotations, regenerate with `swag init -g rest.go`, rebuild the binary, and invalidate CloudFront cache for `/docs/*`
   192→
   193→## Related Documentation
   194→
   195→- [CloudFront Setup Guide](cloudfront-setup.md)
   196→- [SSH Subdomain Setup](ssh-subdomain-setup.md)
   197→- [MCP Server README](../../safecast-map-MCP/README.md)
   198→

     1→# Production Deployment Guide
     2→
     3→This guide covers deploying to the production server at simplemap.safecast.org.
     4→
     5→## Infrastructure Overview
     6→
     7→**Domain:** simplemap.safecast.org
     8→**Server IP:** 65.108.24.131
     9→**CDN:** AWS CloudFront (Distribution ID: E12FYIQ8RRXOJ1)
    10→
    11→### Traffic Flow
    12→
    13→```
    14→┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
    15→│                   Web Traffic (Ports 80/443)            │
    16→└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
    17→  User Browser
    18→       ↓
    19→  simplemap.safecast.org (DNS → CloudFront)
    20→       ↓
    21→  CloudFront Edge Locations (Global CDN)
    22→       ↓
    23→  Origin Server: 65.108.24.131
    24→
    25→
    26→┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
    27→│              Deployment Traffic (Port 22)               │
    28→└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
    29→  Developer Machine
    30→       ↓
    31→  SSH/Rsync to 65.108.24.131 (Direct IP)
    32→       ↓
    33→  Server: 65.108.24.131
    34→```
    35→
    36→## ⚠️ Critical: SSH Must Use IP Address
    37→
    38→**CloudFront only handles HTTP/HTTPS traffic.** SSH connections MUST go directly to the server IP.
    39→
    40→**✅ Correct:**
    41→```bash
    42→ssh -i ~/.ssh/safecast-deploy root@65.108.24.131
    43→```
    44→
    45→**❌ Wrong (will fail):**
    46→```bash
    47→ssh -i ~/.ssh/safecast-deploy root@simplemap.safecast.org  # CloudFront can't handle SSH!
    48→```
    49→
    50→## Manual Deployment
    51→
    52→### Prerequisites
    53→
    54→1. **SSH Key:** `~/.ssh/safecast-deploy` (private key)
    55→2. **Binary built:** `go build -o safecast-new-map .`
    56→3. **Server access:** Ability to SSH to 65.108.24.131
    57→
    58→### Deployment Steps (Always Use This Order)
    59→
    60→**Step 1: Stop the service**
    61→```bash
    62→ssh -i ~/.ssh/safecast-deploy root@65.108.24.131 "systemctl stop safecast-new-map"
    63→```
    64→
    65→**Step 2: Sync the binary**
    66→```bash
    67→rsync -avP -e "ssh -i ~/.ssh/safecast-deploy" ./safecast-new-map root@65.108.24.131:/usr/local/bin/
    68→```
    69→
    70→**Step 3: Start the service**
    71→```bash
    72→ssh -i ~/.ssh/safecast-deploy root@65.108.24.131 "systemctl start safecast-new-map"
    73→```
    74→
    75→**Step 4: Verify deployment**
    76→```bash
    77→ssh -i ~/.ssh/safecast-deploy root@65.108.24.131 "systemctl status safecast-new-map"
    78→```
    79→
    80→**Step 5: Invalidate CloudFront cache (optional)**
    81→```bash
    82→aws cloudfront create-invalidation --distribution-id E12FYIQ8RRXOJ1 --paths "/*"
    83→```
    84→
    85→### Why This Order Matters
    86→
    87→1. **Stop first:** Prevents file conflicts when replacing the binary
    88→2. **Sync binary:** Upload new version while service is stopped
    89→3. **Start service:** New version loads cleanly
    90→4. **Verify:** Ensure service started successfully
    91→
    92→## Automated Deployment (GitHub Actions)
    93→
    94→**Current workflow:** `.github/workflows/deploy.yml`
    95→
    96→### How It Works
    97→
    98→1. **Trigger:** Automatic on push to `main` branch, or manual via workflow_dispatch
    99→2. **Build:** Compiles Go binary on GitHub runners
   100→3. **Deploy:** Uses SSH with stored private key to deploy to 65.108.24.131
   101→4. **Invalidate:** Clears CloudFront cache to serve new version
   102→
   103→### Required GitHub Secrets
   104→
   105→| Secret | Value | Purpose |
   106→|--------|-------|---------|
   107→| `DEPLOY_SSH_KEY` | Content of `~/.ssh/safecast-deploy` | SSH authentication |
   108→| `AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID` | AWS access key | CloudFront invalidation |
   109→| `AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY` | AWS secret key | CloudFront invalidation |
   110→
   111→### Workflow Steps
   112→
   113→```yaml
   114→1. Checkout code
   115→2. Set up Go 1.23
   116→3. Build binary
   117→4. Setup SSH (using 65.108.24.131 IP)
   118→5. Stop service
   119→6. Deploy binary via rsync
   120→7. Start service
   121→8. Verify deployment
   122→9. Invalidate CloudFront cache
   123→10. Cleanup SSH keys
   124→```
   125→
   126→**Note:** The workflow correctly uses the IP address (65.108.24.131) for all SSH operations.
   127→
   128→## CloudFront Considerations
   129→
   130→### Cache Invalidation
   131→
   132→After deploying new code, CloudFront may serve cached versions of static files. Create an invalidation to force cache refresh:
   133→
   134→```bash
   135→# Invalidate all cached content
   136→aws cloudfront create-invalidation --distribution-id E12FYIQ8RRXOJ1 --paths "/*"
   137→
   138→# Invalidate specific paths
   139→aws cloudfront create-invalidation --distribution-id E12FYIQ8RRXOJ1 --paths "/index.html" "/static/*"
   140→```
   141→
   142→**Cost:** First 1,000 invalidation paths per month are free, then $0.005 per path.
   143→
   144→### Cache Behavior
   145→
   146→Current configuration:
   147→- **API endpoints:** No caching (Cache-Control headers prevent it)
   148→- **Static files:** Cached at edge locations
   149→- **Uploads:** Not cached (Cache-Control: private)
   150→- **Admin pages:** Not cached (Cache-Control: no-store)
   151→
   152→### WAF Configuration
   153→
   154→AWS WAF protects against:
   155→- SQL injection
   156→- Cross-site scripting (XSS)
   157→- ~~Large request bodies~~ (Changed to "Count" mode for file uploads)
   158→
   159→**Important:** `SizeRestrictions_BODY` rule is in "Count" mode to allow large file uploads. See [docs/cloudfront-fix-waf-403.md](cloudfront-fix-waf-403.md) for details.
   160→
   161→## Server Configuration
   162→
   163→### Service Details
   164→
   165→**Service name:** `safecast-new-map`
   166→**Binary location:** `/usr/local/bin/safecast-new-map`
   167→**Service file:** `/etc/systemd/system/safecast-new-map.service`
   168→
   169→### Useful Commands
   170→
   171→```bash
   172→# View service status
   173→ssh -i ~/.ssh/safecast-deploy root@65.108.24.131 "systemctl status safecast-new-map"
   174→
   175→# View logs
   176→ssh -i ~/.ssh/safecast-deploy root@65.108.24.131 "journalctl -u safecast-new-map -f"
   177→
   178→# Restart service
   179→ssh -i ~/.ssh/safecast-deploy root@65.108.24.131 "systemctl restart safecast-new-map"
   180→
   181→# Check disk space
   182→ssh -i ~/.ssh/safecast-deploy root@65.108.24.131 "df -h"
   183→
   184→# Check memory usage
   185→ssh -i ~/.ssh/safecast-deploy root@65.108.24.131 "free -h"
   186→```
   187→
   188→## Troubleshooting
   189→
   190→### SSH Connection Fails
   191→
   192→**Symptom:** `ssh: connect to host simplemap.safecast.org port 22: Connection refused`
   193→
   194→**Cause:** Trying to SSH to the domain name instead of IP address.
   195→
   196→**Solution:** Use IP address: `65.108.24.131`
   197→
   198→### Deployment Succeeds But Changes Not Visible
   199→
   200→**Cause:** CloudFront is serving cached content.
   201→
   202→**Solution:** Invalidate CloudFront cache (see above).
   203→
   204→### Upload 403 Errors After Deployment
   205→
   206→**Cause:** WAF blocking large uploads or missing cache headers.
   207→
   208→**Solution:**
   209→1. Verify WAF rules are in "Count" mode (see [cloudfront-fix-waf-403.md](cloudfront-fix-waf-403.md))
   210→2. Check Cache-Control headers in code (safecast-new-map.go)
   211→
   212→### Service Won't Start
   213→
   214→```bash
   215→# Check service status
   216→ssh -i ~/.ssh/safecast-deploy root@65.108.24.131 "systemctl status safecast-new-map"
   217→
   218→# View recent logs
   219→ssh -i ~/.ssh/safecast-deploy root@65.108.24.131 "journalctl -u safecast-new-map -n 50"
   220→
   221→# Check if port is already in use
   222→ssh -i ~/.ssh/safecast-deploy root@65.108.24.131 "netstat -tulpn | grep 8765"
   223→```
   224→
   225→## Rollback Procedure
   226→
   227→If deployment fails:
   228→
   229→```bash
   230→# Stop broken version
   231→ssh -i ~/.ssh/safecast-deploy root@65.108.24.131 "systemctl stop safecast-new-map"
   232→
   233→# Restore previous binary (if backed up)
   234→ssh -i ~/.ssh/safecast-deploy root@65.108.24.131 "cp /usr/local/bin/safecast-new-map.old /usr/local/bin/safecast-new-map"
   235→
   236→# Start service
   237→ssh -i ~/.ssh/safecast-deploy root@65.108.24.131 "systemctl start safecast-new-map"
   238→```
   239→
   240→## Security Notes
   241→
   242→### SSH Key Management
   243→
   244→- **Private key:** Keep `~/.ssh/safecast-deploy` secure and never commit to git
   245→- **GitHub Secret:** Stored encrypted in GitHub, only accessible to workflows
   246→- **Server:** Public key in `/root/.ssh/authorized_keys` on 65.108.24.131
   247→
   248→### CloudFront Security
   249→
   250→- **HTTPS only:** HTTP requests redirected to HTTPS
   251→- **WAF enabled:** Protects against common web attacks
   252→- **DDoS protection:** AWS Shield Standard included with CloudFront
   253→- **Origin protection:** Origin server (65.108.24.131) can be firewalled to only accept CloudFront IPs
   254→

{

  "old_string": "## Overview\n\nThe MCP server and map server both run behind CloudFront, with nginx routing requests to the correct backend based on the URL path.\n\n## Architecture\n\n```\nUser Request\n    ↓\nCloudFront (simplemap.safecast.org)\n    ↓ HTTPS (port 443)\norigin-simplemap.safecast.org\n    ↓\nNginx (routes by path)\n    ├─ /mcp-http → MCP Server (port 3333)\n    ├─ /mcp      → MCP Server (port 3333)\n    ├─ /docs/    → MCP Server (port 3333)\n    ├─ /api/mcp/ → MCP Server (port 3333)\n    └─ /         → Map Server (port 8765)\n```",
  "new_string": "## Overview\n\nAll three services (map server, MCP server, web-chat assistant) live in the **same repository** (`safecast-new-map`) and are built and deployed together via a single GitHub Actions workflow. They run behind CloudFront, with nginx routing requests to the correct backend based on the URL path.\n\n## Repository Structure\n\n```\nsafecast-new-map/\n├── safecast-new-map.go   # Map server (port 8765)\n├── cmd/\n│   ├── mcp-server/       # MCP + REST API server (port 3333)\n│   └── web-chat/         # Claude AI chat UI (port 3334)\n└── .github/workflows/\n    └── deploy.yml        # Builds and deploys all 3 binaries\n```\n\n## Architecture\n\n```\nUser Request\n    ↓\nCloudFront (simplemap.safecast.org)\n    ↓ HTTPS (port 443)\norigin-simplemap.safecast.org\n    ↓\nNginx (routes by path)\n    ├─ /mcp-http         → MCP Server (port 3333)\n    ├─ /mcp              → MCP Server (port 3333)\n    ├─ /docs/            → MCP Server (port 3333) — Swagger UI\n    ├─ /assistant/       → Web-chat (port 3334)\n    ├─ /api/radiation    → MCP Server (port 3333)\n    ├─ /api/area         → MCP Server (port 3333)\n    ├─ /api/sensors      → MCP Server (port 3333)\n    ├─ /api/sensor/      → MCP Server (port 3333)\n    ├─ /api/device/      → MCP Server (port 3333)\n    ├─ /api/spectra      → MCP Server (port 3333)\n    ├─ /api/stats        → MCP Server (port 3333)\n    ├─ /api/extreme      → MCP Server (port 3333)\n    ├─ /api/info/        → MCP Server (port 3333)\n    ├─ /api/gpt/         → MCP Server (port 3333)\n    ├─ /api/track/       → MCP Server (port 3333)\n    └─ /                 → Map Server (port 8765) — everything else\n                           including /api/auth/, /api/user/,\n                           /api/admin/, /api/spectrum/, /api/markers/\n```\n\n> **Important:** The nginx config uses specific location blocks for MCP endpoints rather than a broad `/api/` rule. This ensures that map-server API paths (`/api/auth/`, `/api/spectrum/`, etc.) are not accidentally routed to the MCP server."
}

  "new_string": "### 2. Nginx Configuration\n\n**File:** `/etc/nginx/sites-available/origin-simplemap.safecast.org`\n\nThis is the config that CloudFront hits (via `origin-simplemap.safecast.org`). It uses **specific** location blocks for each MCP endpoint — a broad `/api/` rule would break map-server routes like `/api/auth/` and `/api/spectrum/`.\n\n```nginx\nserver {\n    listen 443 ssl http2;\n    server_name origin-simplemap.safecast.org;\n\n    # MCP protocol transports\n    location /mcp-http { proxy_pass http://localhost:3333; ... }\n    location /mcp      { proxy_pass http://localhost:3333; proxy_http_version 1.1; ... }\n\n    # Swagger UI\n    location /docs/ { proxy_pass http://localhost:3333/docs/; ... }\n\n    # Web-chat assistant\n    location /assistant/ { proxy_pass http://localhost:3334/; ... }\n\n    # Map server auth/user/admin (must come before catch-all)\n    location /api/auth/  { proxy_pass http://localhost:8765; ... }\n    location /api/user/  { proxy_pass http://localhost:8765; ... }\n    location /api/admin/ { proxy_pass http://localhost:8765; client_max_body_size 100M; ... }\n\n    # MCP REST API — specific endpoints only\n    location = /api/radiation { proxy_pass http://localhost:3333/api/radiation; ... }\n    location = /api/area      { proxy_pass http://localhost:3333/api/area; ... }\n    location = /api/sensors   { proxy_pass http://localhost:3333/api/sensors; ... }\n    location /api/sensor/     { proxy_pass http://localhost:3333/api/sensor/; ... }\n    location /api/device/     { proxy_pass http://localhost:3333/api/device/; ... }\n    location = /api/spectra   { proxy_pass http://localhost:3333/api/spectra; ... }\n    location = /api/stats     { proxy_pass http://localhost:3333/api/stats; ... }\n    location = /api/extreme   { proxy_pass http://localhost:3333/api/extreme; ... }\n    location /api/info/       { proxy_pass http://localhost:3333/api/info/; ... }\n    location /api/gpt/        { proxy_pass http://localhost:3333/api/gpt/; ... }\n    location /api/track/      { proxy_pass http://localhost:3333/api/track/; ... }\n\n    # Map server — everything else (including /api/spectrum/, /api/markers/, etc.)\n    location / {\n        proxy_pass http://localhost:8765;\n        client_max_body_size 100M;\n        ...\n    }\n}\n```"
}

This breaks map-server routes (`/api/auth/`, `/api/spectrum/`, etc.). Always use specific `location = /api/endpoint` or `location /api/prefix/` rules for MCP endpoints.\n\n## Related Documentation\n\n- [CloudFront Setup Guide](cloudfront-setup.md)\n- [SSH Subdomain Setup](ssh-subdomain-setup.md)\n- [Deployment Guide](DEPLOYMENT.md)"
